	available_if_capitulated = no

	x = 8
	y = 0
	select_effect = {
		ITA = { #setup event for Iraq stability timed so it appears 90 days before the war

				id = ITA_med.2005
				days = 86
			}
		}

			#STRIP(dead_modifier) }

shared_focus = {
	id = TUR_flowers_on_the_hills
	icon = GFX_TUR_Flower_On_The_Hills
	cost = 2
	available_if_capitulated = no
	ai_will_do = { factor = 1 }
	available = {
		NOT = { has_war_with = IID }
	}
	prerequisite = {
		focus = TUR_the_holy_city_out_of_reach
		focus = TUR_the_levant_subjugated
	}
	prerequisite = {
		focus = TUR_greece_holds
		focus = TUR_mutilation_progress
	}
	x = 7
	y = 6
	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus TUR_flowers_on_the_hills"
		clr_global_flag = TUR_ITW_soon
		custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_flowers_on_the_hills_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { TUR_ITW_win_counter > 0 }
				}
				country_event = { id = tur.80 days = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { TUR_ITW_win_counter > 0 } }
				}
				country_event = { id = tur.81 days = 1 }
			}
			
			#add DMZs back if removed by war
			if = { #total loss against greece DMZ
				limit = { 1266 = { is_owned_by = GRE } }
				1267 = { set_demilitarized_zone = yes }
			}
			else_if = { #loss against greece DMZ
				limit = { 184 = { is_owned_by = GRE } }
				1266 = { set_demilitarized_zone = yes }
			}
			else_if = { #minor loss against greece DMZ
				limit = { has_country_flag = TUR_GTW_lost }
				184 = { set_demilitarized_zone = yes }
				1266 = { set_demilitarized_zone = yes }
			}

			#Undo the martial law from Report For Reform
			if = { 
				limit = { has_country_flag = tur_dts_kurdish_martial_law_during_itw }
				352 = { clr_state_flag = DtS_No_Revolt_Martial_Law }
				1369 = { clr_state_flag = DtS_No_Revolt_Martial_Law }
				781 = { clr_state_flag = DtS_No_Revolt_Martial_Law }
			}
		}
	}
}
